PS C:\Users\Laco> try {
    Write-Output "===== TEST-46: SPP ACTIVATION EVENT XML ====="
    Write-Output "TIMESTAMP: $(Get-Date -Format o)"
    Write-Output "WINDOW START: $since"

    $events = @(
        Get-WinEvent -LogName Application -MaxEvents 6000 |
        Where-Object {
            $_.TimeCreated -ge $since -and
            $_.ProviderName -match 'Security-SPP'
        } |
        Sort-Object TimeCreated
    )

    Write-Output "`n===== SPP EVENT INVENTORY ====="
    $events |
        Select-Object TimeCreated, Id, RecordId,
            LevelDisplayName, ProviderName |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

    $activationEvents = @(
        $events | Where-Object { $_.Id -eq 8198 }
    )

    Write-Output "`n===== 8198 EVENT COUNT ====="
    Write-Output $activationEvents.Count

    Write-Output "`n===== FULL XML: ALL EVENT 8198 RECORDS ====="
    foreach ($event in $activationEvents) {
        Write-Output "`n--- EVENT 8198 ---"
        Write-Output "TIME: $($event.TimeCreated.ToString('o'))"
        Write-Output "RECORD ID: $($event.RecordId)"
        Write-Output $event.ToXml()
    }

    Write-Output "`n===== RELATED SPP EVENTS ====="
    $related = @(
        $events | Where-Object {
            $_.Id -in 1003, 8230, 900, 16384, 16394
        }
    )

    foreach ($event in $related) {
        Write-Output "`n--- EVENT $($event.Id) ---"
        Write-Output "TIME: $($event.TimeCreated.ToString('o'))"
        Write-Output "RECORD ID: $($event.RecordId)"
        Write-Output $event.ToXml()
    }

    Write-Output "`n===== SPP EVENTS WITHIN 2 MINUTES OF EACH 8198 ====="
    $nearby = @(
        foreach ($failure in $activationEvents) {
            $events | Where-Object {
                $_.RecordId -ne $failure.RecordId -and
                [math]::Abs(($_.TimeCreated - $failure.TimeCreated).TotalSeconds) -le 120
            }
        }
    ) | Sort-Object RecordId -Unique

    foreach ($event in $nearby) {
        Write-Output "`n--- NEARBY EVENT $($event.Id) ---"
        Write-Output "TIME: $($event.TimeCreated.ToString('o'))"
        Write-Output "RECORD ID: $($event.RecordId)"
        Write-Output $event.ToXml()
    }

    Write-Output "`n===== AVAILABLE SPP-RELATED EVENT CHANNELS ====="
    & wevtutil.exe el |
        Where-Object { $_ -match 'SPP|SoftwareProtection' }

    Write-Output "`n===== END TEST-46 ====="
}
catch {
    Write-Output "TEST-46 ERROR: $($_.Exception.Message)"
}
